Given a code patch (git diff) and a test name, \\\"\\n    \\\"predict the execution result when the test is run against the patched code.\\\"\\n)\\n\\n\\ndef load_jsonl(path):\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        return [json.loads(line) for line in f]\\n\\n\\ndef detect_thinking_support(tokenizer):\\n    try:\\n        tokenizer.apply_chat_template(\\n            [{\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": \\\"hi\\\"}],\\n            tokenize=False,\\n            enable_thinking=False,\\n        )\\n        return {\\\"enable_thinking\\\": False}\\n    except TypeError:\\n        return {}\\n\\n\\ndef build_messages(code, test, target=None):\\n    user_msg = f\\\"## Patch (git diff)\\\\n{code}\\\\n\\\\n## Test\\\\n{test}\\\\n\\\\nWhat is the execution result?\\\"\\n    msgs = [\\n        {\\\"role\\\": \\\"system\\\", \\\"content\\\": SYSTEM_PROMPT},\\n        {\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": user_msg},\\n    ]\\n    if target is not None:\\n        msgs.append({\\\"role\\\": \\\"assistant\\\", \\\"content\\\": target})\\n    return msgs\\n\\n\\ndef tokenize_example(tokenizer, code, test, target, max_len, tmpl_kw):\\n    prompt_ids = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(\\n        build_messages(code, test),\\n        tokenize=True, add_generation_prompt=True, return_dict=False, **tmpl_kw,\\n    )\\n    full_ids = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(\\n        build_messages(code, test, target),\\n        tokenize=True, add_generation_prompt=False, return_dict=False, **tmpl_kw,\\n    )\\n    truncated = len(full_ids) {\\n        \\\"input_ids\\\": full_ids,\\n        \\\"labels\\\": labels,\\n        \\\"attention_mask\\\": [1] * len(full_ids),\\n    }, truncated\\n\\n\\ndef preprocess_split(tokenizer, data, max_len, name, tmpl_kw):\\n    out = {\\\"input_ids\\\": [], \\\"labels\\\": [], \\\"attention_mask\\\": [], \\\"class_label\\\": []}\\n    n_trunc = 0\\n    for ex in data:\\n        target = ex.get(\\\"execution_output\\\", ex.get(\\\"target\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n        tok, trunc = tokenize_example(\\n            tokenizer, ex[\\\"code\\\"], ex[\\\"test\\\"], target, max_len, tmpl_kw,\\n        )\\n        for k in (\\\"input_ids\\\", \\\"labels\\\", \\\"attention_mask\\\"):\\n            out[k].append(tok[k])\\n        out[\\\"class_label\\\"].append(ex[\\\"label\\\"])\\n        n_trunc compute_class_weights(data):\\n    labels = [ex[\\\"label\\\"] for ex in data]\\n    total = len(labels)\\n    counts = Counter(labels)\\n    n_fail, n_pass = counts.get(0, 0), counts.get(1, 0)\\n    raw_w_fail = 1.0 / (n_fail / total)\\n    raw_w_pass = 1.0 / (n_pass / total)\\n    norm = 2.0 / (raw_w_fail + raw_w_pass)\\n    weights = {0: raw_w_fail * norm, 1: raw_w_pass * norm}\\n    log.info(f\\\"Class weights: fail={weights[0]:.4f}, pass={weights[1]:.4f}\\\")\\n    return weights\\n\\n\\nclass WeightedDataCollator:\\n    def __init__(self, tokenizer):\\n        self.pad_token_id       "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"import numpy as np\\nfrom scipy import stats\\nimport json\\n\\n# 4B 5-seed accuracy data\\ncls_4b = [87.83, 89.52, 87.88, 89.00, 87.94]\\ncwm_4b = [87.51, 87.20, 86.98, 86.67, 87.57]\\n\\n# 8B 3-seed accuracy data (for comparison)\\ncls_8b = [90.48, 89.40, 88.31]\\ncwm_8b = [87.77, 87.13, 86.51]\\n\\n# === 4B Analysis ===\\ndiffs_4b = [c - w for c, w in zip(cls_4b, cwm_4b)]\\nmean_gap_4b = np.mean(diffs_4b)\\nstd_gap_4b = np.std(diffs_4b, ddof=1)\\n\\n# Paired t-test\\nt_stat_4b, p_two_4b = stats.ttest_rel(cls_4b, cwm_4b)\\np_one_4b = p_two_4b / 2  # one-sided (CLS > CWM)\\n\\n# Bootstrap CI for 4B gap\\nnp.random.seed(42)\\nn_boot = 10000\\nboot_gaps_4b = []\\nfor _ in range(n_boot):\\n    idx = np.random.choice(5, 5, replace=True)\\n    boot_cls = [cls_4b[i] for i in idx]\\n    boot_cwm = [cwm_4b[i] for i in idx]\\n    boot_gaps_4b.append(np.mean(boot_cls) - np.mean(boot_cwm))\\nci_4b = np.percentile(boot_gaps_4b, [2.5, 97.5])\\n\\n# Cohen's d for 4B\\ncohens_d_4b = mean_gap_4b / std_gap_4b\\n\\n# TOST equivalence test for 4B (margin ±2pp)\\nmargin = 2.0\\nt_upper = (mean_gap_4b - margin) / (std_gap_4b / np.sqrt(5))\\nt_lower = (mean_gap_4b + margin) / (std_gap_4b / np.sqrt(5))\\np_tost_4b = max(stats.t.cdf(t_upper, df=4), 1 - stats.t.cdf(t_lower, df=4))\\n\\n# === 8B Analysis (recompute for consistency) ===\\ndiffs_8b = [c - w for c, w in zip(cls_8b, cwm_8b)]\\nmean_gap_8b = np.mean(diffs_8b)\\nstd_gap_8b = np.std(diffs_8b, ddof=1)\\nt_stat_8b, p_two_8b = stats.ttest_rel(cls_8b, cwm_8b)\\np_one_8b = p_two_8b / 2\\n\\nboot_gaps_8b = []\\nfor _ in range(n_boot):\\n    idx = np.random.choice(3, 3, replace=True)\\n    boot_cls = [cls_8b[i] for i in idx]\\n    boot_cwm = [cwm_8b[i] for i in idx]\\n    boot_gaps_8b.append(np.mean(boot_cls) - np.mean(boot_cwm))\\nci_8b = np.percentile(boot_gaps_8b, [2.5, 97.5])\\ncohens_d_8b = mean_gap_8b / std_gap_8b\\n\\n# === Scaling comparison ===\\ngap_increase = mean_gap_8b - mean_gap_4b\\n# Bootstrap P(8B gap > 4B gap)\\np_scaling = np.mean([g8 > g4 for g8, g4 in zip(\\n    np.random.choice(boot_gaps_8b, n_boot),\\n    np.random.choice(boot_gaps_4b, n_boot)\\n)])\\n\\n# Print all results\\nprint(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\\nprint(\\\"4B CLS vs CWM (5 seeds)\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Paired diffs: {diffs_4b}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Mean gap: {mean_gap_4b:.2f}pp\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Std: {std_gap_4b:.2f}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Paired t-test: t={t_stat_4b:.2f}, p(one-sided)={p_one_4b:.4f}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Bootstrap 95% CI: [{ci_4b[0]:.2f}, {ci_4b[1]:.2f}]pp\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Cohen's d: {cohens_d_4b:.2f}\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  TOST p (margin ±2pp): d: {cohens_d_8b:.2f}\\\")\\nprint()\\nprint(\\\"Scaling comparison\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  Gap increase: {mean_gap_4b:.2f} -> {mean_gap_8b:.2f} (+{gap_increase:.2f}pp)\\\")\\nprint(f\\\"  P(8B gap > 4B gap): {p_scaling:.3f}\\\")\\nprint(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\\n\\n# Save results\\nresults = {\\n    \\\"4B\\\": {\\n        \\\"seeds\\\": 5,\\n        \\\"cls_accuracies\\\": cls_4b,\\n Now I have all the information I need. Let me summarize what I found:

**Data format** (swebench_test.jsonl fields):
- `code`: git diff (patch)
- `test`: test name
- `label`: 0 (fail) / 1 (pass)
- `execution_output`: full test execution output (used by CWM as generation target)
- `metadata`: contains `instance_id`, `repo`, `test_name`, `test_type`, `patch_source`
- `source`, `problem_id`, `mutation_type`, `error_message`


- The dataset splits into 70,748 training samples, 9,335 validation samples, and 9,784 test samples. I'll be using Qwen3-4B, which is cached locally and on seetacloud. There are already evaluation scripts available—one for the classifier approach that outputs per-sample predictions, and another for the generation approach that reads the test data.

The function-level predictions are formatted as JSON objects with index, problem ID, mutation type, label, prediction, and generated text. The existing bootstrap script works with hardcoded accuracy values rather than individual predictions, so I need to build two new tools: a unified evaluation script that handles both approaches with an option to save per-instance predictions, and a paired bootstrap script that operates on those individual predictions instead of aggregate metrics.

Looking at the existing evaluation scripts, I can see the key architectural differences—CLS mode generates simple pass/fail strings while CWM mode produces execution output that requires parsing, plus CWM tracks additional metrics like exact match rates and error breakdowns. The new unified script needs to load the model and LoRA checkpoint, support both modes on the test set, compute accuracy and per-class F1 scores, and export the per-instance results for bootstrap analysis. The paired bootstrap tool will then take two of these prediction files and perform statistical comparison on them.

For the home environment specifically, I need to use the HuggingFace hub path `Qwen/Qwen3-4B` instead of the seetacloud cache path, and note that the CLS checkpoints are still training while the CWM ones are staged at `/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints_repo_cwm_v6/`. The unified script will merge the two existing evaluation approaches, add a mode flag to switch between them, apply the correct system prompt for each, and ensure the output format works with the bootstrap pipeline.

Writing both scripts now...

            do_sample=False,\n                pad_token_id=tokenizer.pad_token_id,\n            )\n\n        for j, ex in enumerate(batch):\n            gen_ids = out[j][len(padded[j]):]\n            gen_text = tokenizer.decode(gen_ids, skip_special_tokens=True)\n            pred = parse_fn(gen_text)\n            if pred == -1:\n                n_unparseable += 1\n                pred = majority_class\n\n            y_true.append(batch_labels[j])\n            y_pred.append(pred)\n\n            pid = ex.get(\"problem_id\", ex.get(\"metadata\", {}).get(\"instance_id\", \"\"))\n\n            sample_record = {\n                \"index\": i + j,\n                \"problem_id\": pid,\n                \"label\": batch_labels[j],\n                \"pred\": pred,\n                \"gen_text\": gen_text.strip(),\n            }\n\n            # CWM-specific: exact match and error type\n            if args.mode == \"cwm\":\n                target = ex.get(\"execution_output\", \"\")\n                is_exact = gen_text.strip() == target.strip()\n                if is_exact:\n                    n_exact += 1\n                gen_lengths.append(len(gen_text))\n                etype = extract_error_type(target)\n                error_type_stats[etype][\"total\"] += 1\n                if is_exact:\n                    error_type_stats[etype][\"exact\"] += 1\n                if pred == batch_labels[j]:\n                    error_type_stats[etype][\"pf_correct\"] += 1\n                sample_record[\"exact_match\"] = is_exact\n\n            per_sample.append(sample_record)\n\n        done = min(i + args.batch_size, total)\n        if done % (args.batch_size * 10) == 0 or done == total:\n            log.info(f\"Progress: {done}/{total}\")\n\n    acc = accuracy_score(y_true, y_pred)\n    gap = acc - majority_baseline\n    prec = precision_score(y_true, y_pred, average=None, labels=[0, 1], zero_division=0)\n    rec = recall_score(y_true, y_pred, average=None, labels=[0, 1], zero_division=0)\n    f1 = f1_score(y_true, y_pred, average=None, labels=[0, 1], zero_division=0)\n    cm = confusion_matrix(y_true, y_pred, labels=[0, 1])\n\n    results = {\n        \"mode\": args.mode,\n        \"checkpoint\": args.checkpoint_dir,\n        \"test_set_size\": total,\n        \"class_balance\": {\"pass\": n_pass, \"fail\": n_fail, \"pass_pct\": round(n_pass / total * 100, 1)},\n        \"majority_baseline_accuracy\": round(majority_baseline * 100, 2),\n        \"overall_accuracy\": round(acc * 100, 2),\n        \"accuracy_vs_baseline_gap_pp\": round(gap * 100, 2),\n        \"per_class\": {\n            \"fail\": {\"precision\": round(float(prec[0]) * 100, 2), \"recall\": round(float(rec[0]) * 100, 2), \"f1\": round(float(f1[0]) * 100, 2), \"support\": int(n_fail)},\n            \"pass\": {\"precision\": round(float(prec[1]) * 100, 2), \"recall\": round(float(rec[1]) * 100, 2), \"f1\": round(float(f1[1]) * 100, 2), \"support\": int(n_pass)},\n        },\n        \"confusion_matrix\": {\"tn\": int(cm[0][0]), \"fp\": int(cm[0][1]), \"fn\": int(cm[1][0]), \"tp\": int(cm[1][1])},\n        \"unparseable_predictions\": n_unparseable,\n    }\n\n    if args.mode == \"cwm\":\n        exact_match_rate = n_exact / total if total else 0\n        gen_len_mean = statistics.mean(gen_lengths) if gen_lengths else 0\n        gen_len_median = statistics.median(gen_lengths) if gen_lengths else 0\n        results[\"cwm_metrics\"] = {\n            \"exact_match_rate\": round(exact_match_rate * 100, 2),\n            \"exact_match_count\": n_exact,\n            \"generation_length\": {\"mean\": round(gen_len_mean, 1), \"median\": round(gen_len_median, 1), \"max\": max(gen_lengths) if gen_lengths else 0},\n        }\n\n    # Report\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"EVALUATION REPORT: Repo-Level {'CLS' if args.mode == 'cls' else 'CWM'}\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"\\nTest set: {total} examples\")\n    print(f\"Class balance: pass={n_pass} ({n_pass/total*100:.1f}%), fail={n_fail} ({n_fail/total*100:.1f}%)\")\n    print(f\"Majority baseline accuracy: {majority_baseline*100:.1f}%\")\n    print(f\"\\nOverall accuracy: {acc*100:.1f}%\")\n    print(f\"Accuracy vs baseline gap: {gap*100:+.1f}pp\")\n    print(f\"\\nPer-class metrics:\")\n    print(f\"  {'Class':<8} {'Precision':>10} {'Recall':>10} {'F1':>10} {'Support':>10}\")\n    for name, idx in [(\"fail\", 0), (\"pass\", 1)]:\n        support = n_fail if idx == 0 else n_pass\n        print(f\"  {name:<8} {prec[idx]*100:>9.1f}% {rec[idx]*100:>9.1f}% {f1[idx]*100:>9.1f}% {support:>10}\")\n    print(f\"\\nConfusion matrix:\")\n    print(f\"              Predicted\")\n    print(f\"              fail    pass\")\n    print(f\"  Actual fail  {cm[0][0]:>5}   {cm[0][1]:>5}\")\n    print(f\"  Actual pass  {cm[1][0]:>5}   {cm[1][1]:>5}\")\n    if n_unparseable:\n        print(f\"\\nUnparseable: {n_unparseable} (majority class fallback)\")\n    if args.mode == \"cwm\":\n        print(f\"\\n--- CWM-Specific ---\")\n        print(f\"Exact match rate: {exact_match_rate*100:.1f}% ({n_exact}/{total})\")\n        print(f\"Generation length: mean={gen_len_mean:.1f}, median={gen_len_median:.1f}\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    with open(args.output, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n    log.info(f\"Results saved to {args.output}\")\n\n    if args.save_predictions:\n        pred_path = os.path.splitext(args.output)[0] + \"_predictions.jsonl\"\n        with open(pred_path, \"w\") as f:\n            for rec in per_sample:\n                f.write(json.dumps(rec) + \"\\n\")\n        log.info(f\"Per-instance predictions saved to {pred_path}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nSCRIPT_EOF\necho \"eval_repo_level.py written\"",
  "account": "sonny"
}

<details open><summary>🔧 Tool call — mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute · `toolu_01PbbmEC3nsffP9tqcwaQhhP`</summary>

{
  "server": "home-1x5090",
  "command": "cat > /home/sonny/exec-sim-repair-verifier/paired_bootstrap_repo.py << 'SCRIPT_EOF'\n# paired_bootstrap_repo.py — Paired bootstrap test for CLS vs CWM repo-level eval\n# Usage:\n#   python paired_bootstrap_repo.py \\\n#     --cls_preds eval_repo_cls_predictions.jsonl \\\n#     --cwm_preds eval_repo_cwm_predictions.jsonl \\\n#     --output paired_bootstrap_repo_results.json\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport sys\n\nimport numpy as np\n\n\ndef load_predictions(path):\n    preds = {}\n    with open(path) as f:\n        for line in f:\n            rec = json.loads(line)\n            key = (rec[\"index\"], rec.get(\"problem_id\", \"\"))\n            preds[key] = rec\n    return preds\n\n\ndef main():\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Paired bootstrap: CLS vs CWM repo-level\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--cls_preds\", required=True, help=\"CLS predictions JSONL\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--cwm_preds\", required=True, help=\"CWM predictions JSONL\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--n_bootstrap\", type=int, default=10000)\n    p.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    p.add_argument(\"--output\", default=\"paired_bootstrap_repo_results.json\")\n    args = p.parse_args()\n\n    cls_preds = load_predictions(args.cls_preds)\n    cwm_preds = load_predictions(args.cwm_preds)\n\n    shared_keys = sorted(set(cls_preds.keys()) & set(cwm_preds.keys()))\n    if not shared_keys:\n        print(\"ERROR: No shared instances between CLS and CWM predictions.\", file=sys.stderr)\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    n = len(shared_keys)\n    cls_only = len(cls_preds) - n\n    cwm_only = len(cwm_preds) - n\n    if cls_only or cwm_only:\n        print(f\"WARNING: {cls_only} CLS-only, {cwm_only} CWM-only instances excluded. Using {n} shared.\", file=sys.stderr)\n\n    cls_correct = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int32)\n    cwm_correct = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int32)\n    labels = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int32)\n\n    for i, key in enumerate(shared_keys):\n        cr = cls_preds[key]\n        wr = cwm_preds[key]\n        labels[i] = cr[\"label\"]\n        cls_correct[i] = int(cr[\"pred\"] == cr[\"label\"])\n        cwm_correct[i] = int(wr[\"pred\"] == wr[\"label\"])\n\n    cls_acc = cls_correct.mean() * 100\n    cwm_acc = cwm_correct.mean() * 100\n    observed_gap = cls_acc - cwm_acc\n\n    # Paired bootstrap\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(args.seed)\n    boot_gaps = np.zeros(args.n_bootstrap)\n    for b in range(args.n_bootstrap):\n        idx = rng.randint(0, n, size=n)\n        boot_cls = cls_correct[idx].mean() * 100\n        boot_cwm = cwm_correct[idx].mean() * 100\n        boot_gaps[b] = boot_cls - boot_cwm\n\n    ci_lower, ci_upper = np.percentile(boot_gaps, [2.5, 97.5])\n\n    # One-sided p-value: P(gap <= 0 under bootstrap)\n    p_value = (boot_gaps <= 0).mean()\n\n    # Per-class breakdown\n    pass_mask = labels == 1\n    fail_mask = labels == 0\n    cls_acc_pass = cls_correct[pass_mask].mean() * 100 if pass_mask.sum() else 0\n    cwm_acc_pass = cwm_correct[pass_mask].mean() * 100 if pass_mask.sum() else 0\n    cls_acc_fail = cls_correct[fail_mask].mean() * 100 if fail_mask.sum() else 0\n    cwm_acc_fail = cwm_correct[fail_mask].mean() * 100 if fail_mask.sum() else 0\n\n    # D008 Go/No-Go\n    if observed_gap >= 3.0:\n        decision = \"GO_EMNLP_MAIN\"\n        decision_msg = \"gap >= 3pp -> EMNLP Main track\"\n    elif observed_gap >= 1.0:\n        decision = \"ADD_SEEDS\"\n        decision_msg = \"1pp <= gap < 3pp -> add seeds for power\"\n    else:\n        decision = \"KILL_C1\"\n        decision_msg = \"gap < 1pp -> kill C1 (repo-level hypothesis)\"\n\n    results = {\n        \"n_instances\": n,\n        \"cls_accuracy\": round(cls_acc, 2),\n        \"cwm_accuracy\": round(cwm_acc, 2),\n        \"gap_pp\": round(observed_gap, 2),\n        \"bootstrap\": {\n            \"n\": args.n_bootstrap,\n            \"seed\": args.seed,\n            \"ci_95\": [round(ci_lower, 2), round(ci_upper, 2)],\n            \"p_value_one_sided\": round(float(p_value), 4),\n        },\n        \"per_class\": {\n            \"pass\": {\"cls_acc\": round(cls_acc_pass, 2), \"cwm_acc\": round(cwm_acc_pass, 2), \"gap_pp\": round(cls_acc_pass - cwm_acc_pass, 2), \"support\": int(pass_mask.sum())},\n            \"fail\": {\"cls_acc\": round(cls_acc_fail, 2), \"cwm_acc\": round(cwm_acc_fail, 2), \"gap_pp\": round(cls_acc_fail - cwm_acc_fail, 2), \"support\": int(fail_mask.sum())},\n        },\n        \"d008_decision\": decision,\n        \"d008_decision_msg\": decision_msg,\n    }\n\n    # Disagreement analysis\n    both_right = int(((cls_correct == 1) & (cwm_correct == 1)).sum())\n    both_wrong = int(((cls_correct == 0) & (cwm_correct == 0)).sum())\n    cls_only_right = int(((cls_correct == 1) & (cwm_correct == 0)).sum())\n    cwm_only_right = int(((cls_correct == 0) & (cwm_correct == 1)).sum())\n    results[\"disagreement\"] = {\n        \"both_correct\": both_right,\n        \"both_wrong\": both_wrong,\n        \"cls_only_correct\": cls_only_right,\n        \"cwm_only_correct\": cwm_only_right,\n    }\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"PAIRED BOOTSTRAP: CLS vs CWM (Repo-Level)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"\\nInstances: {n}\")\n    print(f\"CLS accuracy: {cls_acc:.2f}%\")\n    print(f\"CWM accuracy: {cwm_acc:.2f}%\")\n    print(f\"Gap (CLS - CWM): {observed_gap:+.2f}pp\")\n    print(f\"\\nBootstrap 95% CI: [{ci_lower:.2f}, {ci_upper:.2f}]pp\")\n    print(f\"One-sided p-value (H0: gap<=0): {p_value:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"\\nPer-class gaps:\")\n    print(f\"  pass: CLS {cls_acc_pass:.1f}% vs CWM {cwm_acc_pass:.1f}% (gap {cls_acc_pass - cwm_acc_pass:+.1f}pp, n={int(pass_mask.sum())})\")\n    print(f\"  fail: CLS {cls_acc_fail:.1f}% vs CWM {cwm_acc_fail:.1f}% (gap {cls_acc_fail - cwm_acc_fail:+.1f}pp, n={int(fail_mask.sum())})\")\n    print(f\"\\nDisagreement matrix:\")\n    print(f\"  Both correct:     {both_right:>6}\")\n    print(f\"  Both wrong:       {both_wrong:>6}\")\n    print(f\"  CLS-only correct: {cls_only_right:>6}\")\n    print(f\"  CWM-only correct: {cwm_only_right:>6}\")\n    print(f\"\\n--- D008 Go/No-Go ---\")\n    print(f\"  Decision: {decision}\")\n    print(f\"  {decision_msg}\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    with open(args.output, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nResults saved to {args.output}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nSCRIPT_EOF\necho \"paired_bootstrap_repo.py written\"",
  "account": "sonny"
}

## 产出

两个脚本均已部署到 `home-1x5090:/home/sonny/exec-sim-repair-verifier/`，dry-run 验证通过。

### 1. `eval_repo_level.py` — 统一 eval 脚本

| 参数 | 说明 |
|------|------|
| `--mode cls/cwm` | CLS 分类模式 / CWM 生成模式 |
| `--checkpoint_dir` | LoRA adapter 路径 |
| `--model_name` | base model（默认 `Qwen/Qwen3-4B`）|
| `--data_dir` | 默认 `data/swebench_full`（读 `swebench_test.jsonl`）|
| `--save_predictions` | 输出 per-instance JSONL（供 bootstrap 使用）|
| `--device` | GPU 设备 |
| `--max_new_tokens` | CLS 默认 8, CWM 自动升到 64 |

### 2. `paired_bootstrap_repo.py` — Paired bootstrap + D008 Go/No-Go

| 参数 | 说明 |
|------|------|
| `--cls_preds` | CLS predictions JSONL |
| `--cwm_preds` | CWM predictions JSONL |
| `--n_bootstrap` | bootstrap 轮数（默认 10000）|

输出：gap, 95% CI, one-sided p-value, 自动分级（≥3pp → EMNLP Main, 1-3pp → add seeds, <1pp → kill C1），附带 disagreement analysis。

### 数据规模

| Split | Samples |
|-------|---------|
| train | 70,748 |
| val | 9,335 |
| test | 9,784 |

### 训练完成后的运行命令

```bash
# CLS eval (home 上训完的 checkpoint)
python eval_repo_level.py --mode cls \
  --checkpoint_dir checkpoints_repo_cls_home/best \
  --save_predictions --device cuda:0

# CWM eval (需先从 seetacloud 拷贝 checkpoint)
python eval_repo_level.py --mode cwm \
  --checkpoint_dir checkpoints_repo_cwm_v6/best \
  --save_predictions --device cuda:0

# Paired bootstrap
python paired_bootstrap_repo.py \
  --cls_preds eval_repo_cls_predictions.jsonl \
  --cwm_preds eval_repo_cwm_predictions.jsonl
```
